SUMMER COURSE
WINTER COURSE
Turf. Summer course 2800m in circumference with 600m run-in. Winter course 2700m with 450m 
run-in. Races up to 1200m testing straight course (1200m races are occasionally run round the turn, 
starting on the Summer course and ending on the Winter course); beyond 1200m anti-clockwise 
round turn. DRAW: Low-numbers draws slightly preferred all races. Wet going can impact on draw in 
races down the straight.

ability.

Computaform is packed with info to help you win!      
p
Here’s how to read all the info . . .
1 Jockey silks.
2 Official saddlecloth number.
3 Horse name.
4 Age,	colour ,	sex,	sire	-	dam	and	dam	sire.
5 Date	foaled	&	date	gelded	if	applicable.
6 T rainer	(training	centre)*(magenta	=	1st	run	with	this	train-
er)	&	horse’s	previous	trainer	if	applicable.
7 Number	of	days	since	last	run	and	since	last	win.	FIRST	RUN	
if	debut	(bullet	denotes	CARDED	TO	RUN	before	date	of	this	
race).	Breeder ,	Owner/s	and	description	of	colours.
8 (a)  Career	record	-	runs,	wins,	2nds,	3rds	&	4ths.
 (b)  Career	earnings.
 (c) 2014	-	runs,	wins,	2nds,	3rds	&	4ths.
 (d)  2015	-	runs,	wins,	2nds,	3rds	&	4ths.
 (e)  Carded	jockey’s	career	record	on	this	horse.	
 (f)   Runs,	wins,	2nds,	3rds	&	4ths	for	this	jockey-trainer			
	 	 partnership	 in	last	12	months.
9 (a)  Runs,	wins,	2nds,	3rds	&	4ths	on	current	surface.
** (b)  Runs,	wins,	2nds,	3rds	&	4ths	on	good/standard	going.
** (c)  Runs,	wins,	2nds,	3rds	&	4ths	on	wet/rain	going.
 (d)  Runs,	wins,	2nds,	3rds	&	4ths	in	headgear	declared	for	
this race.
 (e)  1st	or	2nd	or	3rd	etc	race	after	a	rest	–	shows	what	
number start horse is having since last rested (absence 
of	60	days	or	more	from	races)	or	making	debut.	In	
other	words	Rest	+1	will	be	displayed	if	horse	having	
first career run or first run after break of 60 days or 
more;
	Rest	+2	if	horse’s	second	career	run	or	sec-
ond	run	following	a	rest	etc.	The	runs,	wins,	2nds,	
3rds	and	4ths	shown	are	horse’s	career	record	in	this	
number	run	after	a	rest/debut	previously.	In	example	
above	horse	having	1st	run	after	rest	AND	in	its	1st		
start	after	a	rest	at	previous	stages	in	its	career	it	has	
recorded	a	win	and	a	fourth	from	2	run.	
 (f)  WPR:	Win-place	range	(minimum	and	maximum	
	 	 	 distances	at	which	horse	has	finished	in	first	three).
10 (a)  Runs,	wins,	2nds,	3rds	&	4ths	at	this	course.
 (b)  Runs,	wins,	2nds,	3rds	&	4ths	over	this	distance.	
 (c)  Runs,	wins,	2nds,	3rds	&	4ths	over	this	course	&	distance.
 (d)  Runs,	wins,	2nds,	3rds	&	4ths	in	this	distance	category.
 (e)  Runs,	wins,	2nds,	3rds	&	4ths	in	this	Computaform	class.
 (f)  Best	vs	Ave	–	horse’s	best	time	compared	to	the	Class	
D	average	(+	faster	than	average)	after	times	adjusted	
for	state	of	going,	weight	carried	and	lengths	behind.
11 Shoes,	Computaform	rating,	Speed	rating,	headgear	(any	
change	in	headgear	displayed	in	magenta),	t	=	tongue	tie,	
jockey	(*	apprentice),	weight,	Merit	Rating	&	race	rating	
(the	adjusted	Merit	Rating	taking	weight	conditions	of	race	
into	account)	and	starting	gate	position.
12 Date	of	race	(magenta	=	first	run	after	rest	of	60	days	or	
more).	g	=	1st	run	following	gelding.
13 Course	(n	=	night	racing;	o	=	old	course	at	Kenilworth);	
14 Going - Turf  Going	-	Polytrack/Sand
	G 	=	Good	 	 N	=	Standard/normal
	Y 	=	Yielding	 	 R	=	Rain	affected
	S 	=	Soft
	H 	=	Heavy
15 Race reference number.
16 Race	name,	Computaform	class	&	total	prize	money.
17 Average	merit	rating	of	all	runners	in	race.
18 Distance	and	whether	down	straight	or	round	turn:
	S 	=	Straight
	L 	=	Left-hand	turn
	R 	=	Right-hand	turn
19 Shoes,	headgear	and	t	=	tongue	tie	if	applicable.
20 Official	Merit	Rating.
21 Jockey.
22 Weight	carried	and	apprentice	allowance/overweight	if	
applicable.
23 Draw-number	of	runners.
24 Opening	betting	(Italicised	if	TAB	favourite).
25 Starting	price	(Italicised	if	TAB	favourite).
26 Position	at	800m	mark	and	lengths	behind	or	ahead.
27 Position	at	400m	mark	and	lengths	behind	or	ahead.	
28 Finishing	position	and	lengths	behind	or	won	by.
29 Winner	of	race	or	*runner-up	plus	weight	carried.
30 Winner’s	race	time.
31 400m-to-finish	time	for	this	horse.	R	=	how	time	ranked	in	
comparison
	to	other	runners’	final	400m	times	(example	
4=fourth-fastest	400m-to-finish	time	of	all	horses	in	race).
32 Horse’s	adjusted	time	versus	class	D	average	for	distance.
33 Average	time	per	metre	for	this	horse.
34 Speed	Rating.
35 Number	of	next-start	winners	from	this	race	and	total	num-
ber of runners from this race to have raced subsequently 
(maximum	of	next	90	days).
36 Race comment.
**	 Statistics	from	1	June	2010.
